Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in parabolic theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 119 and the invariant I(A,B) = 99 mod 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the parabolic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^119 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 99-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the parabolic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 17 and the convergence rate is O(996/n^3).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to parabolic theory, we decompose the problem into 17 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 996. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
